## 崇祯松江府志卷之三十四　选举上（进士乡举）

<span style="margin-left: 3em">选举</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">吕东莱云取士科目不见于经然攷之鲁论臯陶伊尹</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">非选举之始乎吾松严助举贤良文学陆康举孝廉陆</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">绩举掾较去弓旌远甚及</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">明仍隋唐进士科独称华选士以此自好莫敢跅𧿇败</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">群而前辈亦相与成就爱惜之以待国家缓急之用即</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">天意亦若偏私偏重于科第诸贤而必不旁出于艸野</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">屠钓之内四科三立何其衮衮相望也夫　明主斋沐</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">焚香求之于禁中又录其名姓扁藏于　天府焚告于</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">祖陵此与成周献贤能之书于王王再拜而受之又加</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">重矣嘻是不独为松告也</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">两汉　六朝</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">贤良文学</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">汉孝武建元元年诏丞相御史列侯中二千石诸侯相</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">举贤良方正直言极谏之士　又诏举贤良文学待以</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">不次之位于是会稽郡举严助</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">汉严助<sub class="note-yuan">擢中大夫后为会稽太守助吴人隐于华亭之严山</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">孝廉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">东汉百官志曰郡太守举孝廉郡口二十万举一人又</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">郡国守相视事未满岁不得察举孝廉以其未久不周</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">知也其重如此故贤良一科奏对辄百余人而孝廉之</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">选文帝诏云万家之县无应令者武帝诏云阖郡不荐</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">一人盖孝廉非有实行可见者不容谬举故耳</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">汉陆康<sub class="note-yuan">字季宁先经郡守庄旻举孝廉至是李肃再举</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">晋陆云<sub class="note-yuan">字士龙年十六举孝廉</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">荐辟</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">两汉仕进之门或公府辟召或郡国荐举或由曹掾积</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">累而升魏晋以来相沿不废然汉制公府辟掾属州郡</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">选曹皆自荐举而自试用之苟非其人非特累衡鉴之</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">明抑且失倚毗之助</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">汉陆绩<sub class="note-yuan">字公纪孙权辟为奏曹掾</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆瑁<sub class="note-yuan">字子璋嘉禾元年公车征拜议郎选曹尚书</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆袆<sub class="note-yuan">字元容赤乌六年征为宿卫郎中</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">晋张翰字季鹰<sub class="note-jiao">校：膺字當鷹字之訛今改]齊王冏辟爲大司馬東曹掾</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆机<sub class="note-yuan">字士衡张华荐之扬骏辟为祭酒</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆晔<sub class="note-yuan">字士光元帝初镇江左辟为祭酒累迁散骑常侍本郡大中</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆纳<sub class="note-yuan">字祖言初辟为镇军大将军武陵王掾</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">梁陆倕<sub class="note-yuan">字佐公年十七举本州秀才刺史</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">唐</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">进士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">隋炀帝建进士科唐因其旧虽与秀才明经俊士并设</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">而进士特荣每一年一试所收不得过二十人开元前</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">未属礼部以考功员外郎主之唐武后自诡文墨乃策</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">问于殿前后遂于省试外别有廷试</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">玄宗开元</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顾道行<sub class="note-yuan">字正平野王孙仕为湖州司功参军</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">肃宗至德二载</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顾况<sub class="note-yuan">字逋翁官著作佐郎</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">况负诗名以省试未得前列为恨试题空梁落鷰</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">泥诗云卷幕差池燕常啣浊水泥为黏朱履迹未</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">等画梁齐旧点痕犹浅新巢缉尚低不缘频上落</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">那得此飞栖</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">代宗大历二年</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆贽<sub class="note-yuan">字敬舆西京试六人又中博学宏词拔萃科仕至宰相</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">唐科试集载陆贽禁中春松诗　阴阴清禁里苍</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">翠满春松雨露恩偏近阳和色更<sub class="note-yuan">一作正</sub>浓高枝分</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">晓日灵韵<sub class="note-yuan">一作虚吹</sub>杂宵钟岚助<sub class="note-yuan">一作香动</sub>炉烟远形疑盖</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">影重愿符千岁寿不羡五株封傥得<sub class="note-yuan">一作长得</sub>回天眷</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">全胜老碧峰西京试题</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">僖宗光启二年</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆扆<sub class="note-yuan">字祥文礼第一人</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">北梦琐言曰僖宗幸梁汴扆随驾至行在为宰相</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">韦昭度所知时已深夏以郑损为主司其年六月</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">状头及第后在翰林暑时苦热同列戏之曰今日</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">好造榜矣</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">赐第</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">唐时有勅赐及第以表特恩者或文学之臣献文别试</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">或正额之外绩奏甚放及第皆称赐第实一时之荣遇</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">也</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顾非熊<sub class="note-yuan">况之子会昌中陈商放榜上怪无非熊名召有司追榜勅使及第</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">明经</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">唐明经之别有五经有三经有二经有三礼有三传此</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">岁举之常永隆间以明经多抄义条亡裨<sub class="note-jiao">校：俾字當裨字之訛今改</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">实用自后试帖十得六以上又开元勅曰明经以帖括</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">为功罕穷旨趣其问大义十条对时务策三首</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">丁公著<sub class="note-yuan">字平子五经及第又通开元礼授集贤校书郎</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顾　谦<sub class="note-yuan">字自修举明</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">经三礼二科</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">征辟</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陆龟蒙<sub class="note-yuan">字鲁望刺史张抟辟以自佐李尉卢携为国召拜左拾遗</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宋</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">进士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宋设进士有一甲至五甲者有一举至十五举者虽同</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">五经学究明经而礼数轻重特异陈舜俞曰礼部贡院</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">试进士因唐故事设香案于堦前主司与举人对拜所</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">坐设位供帐甚盛有司具茶汤饮浆至学究则悉彻帐</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">幕毡席茶浆之类渴饮砚水人皆黔其吻故当时有诗</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">云焚香礼进士彻幕待经生余应制科礼遇犹如此嘉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">祐后礼部试士无所谓五经学究而礼亦杀于前矣舜</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">俞华亭人</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">真宗天禧三年<sub class="note-yuan">王整榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吕谔</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">仁宗天圣二年<sub class="note-yuan">宋郊榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吕询</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">景祐元年<sub class="note-yuan">张唐卿榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吕评</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">庆历六年<sub class="note-yuan">贾黯榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陈舜俞<sub class="note-yuan">嘉祐四年再应材识茂异贤良方正能直言极谏科擢第一</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宋时极重开封籍既易登第又得华选故借籍甚多吾</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">郡多开封贯以此鼌迥云举人本贯难于取解奔辏京</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">毂寓籍充赋以致土著登名甚少交搆喧讼司马光言</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">曰每次科第及第进士皆是国子监开封解送之人则</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">人之常情谁能舍此就彼哉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">嘉祐二年<sub class="note-yuan">章衡榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吕全<sub class="note-yuan">用开</sub>　　　郏亶<sub class="note-yuan">字正夫江东转运封贯</sub>　　　<sub class="note-yuan">判官迁太府丞</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">嘉祐六年<sub class="note-yuan">王俊民榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱伯虎<sub class="note-yuan">字才元用开封贯第五甲第二人朝奉大夫江东运判累赠太子少师</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">嘉祐八年<sub class="note-yuan">许将榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">戴显甫<sub class="note-yuan">一姓钱</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱　巩<sub class="note-yuan">字雄道伯虎从父</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">第三甲第二名</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">英宗治平二年<sub class="note-yuan">彭汝弼榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱伯熊<sub class="note-yuan">字才翁伯虎弟以朝散郎知兴国军转朝奉大夫知莱州再任淮东提举卒累赠宣奉大</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 5em">　<sub class="note-yuan">夫</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">董　乂<sub class="note-yuan">字彦臣江西贯魏王宫教授递</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">迁大理寺卿封华亭开国男</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">神宗熙宁三年亲试举人初用策是日进士就席有司</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">犹循故事给礼部韵及题出乃策问也叶祖洽对言祖</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宗多因循苟简之政陛下即位聿而新之初考为三等</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">覆考为五等令宰相陈升之面读以祖洽为第一先是</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">吴公美名在甲榜竟以叶冠其前考官苏轼疾其阿谀</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">因撰进士策一篇以进</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">熙宁三年<sub class="note-yuan">叶祖洽榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吴公美<sub class="note-yuan">用开封贯</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">李　撰<sub class="note-yuan">彭泽令</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">字子约</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">熙宁六年<sub class="note-yuan">余中榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吕　奎<sub class="note-yuan">用开</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">封贯</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">元丰二年<sub class="note-yuan">时彦榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">柳廷俊<sub class="note-yuan">工部</sub>　　　　章粹<sub class="note-yuan">用建侍郎</sub>　　　　<sub class="note-yuan">宁贯</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">哲宗元祐三年<sub class="note-yuan">李常宁榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吕益柔<sub class="note-yuan">字文刚甲科第二人用开封贯刑部侍郎以朝请大夫显谟阁待制致仕</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">张徽言</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">元祐六年<sub class="note-yuan">马涓榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱绂<sub class="note-yuan">伯虎从子甲科第二人后改名谔官尚书左丞谢恩诗云起家白屋连三榜平地青云第二</sub>　　　　　富开　　　朱之纯<sub class="note-yuan">用开名</sub>　　　　　　　　　<sub class="note-yuan">封贯</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">绍圣元年<sub class="note-yuan">毕渐榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">柳廷杰<sub class="note-yuan">廷俊</sub>　　　　吕佶</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 6em"><sub class="note-yuan">之弟</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">绍圣四年<sub class="note-yuan">何昌言榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">张天材<sub class="note-yuan">改名卿材</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">徽宗崇宁二年<sub class="note-yuan">霍端友榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱纮<sub class="note-yuan">绂弟第三甲第一人</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">崇宁五年<sub class="note-yuan">蔡薿榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">王箎　　　张甸　　　　吕少蒙</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">大观三年<sub class="note-yuan">贾安宅榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">柳约<sub class="note-yuan">廷俊弟</sub>姚彀<sub class="note-yuan">改名</sub>　　张昭</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 5em"><sub class="note-yuan">焯</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陈之元　　黄子服　　　衞上达</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">政和五年<sub class="note-yuan">何桌榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">黄镇　　　黄锾<sub class="note-yuan">镇</sub>　　　衞开<sub class="note-yuan">八行科终</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 5em">　　　<sub class="note-yuan">弟</sub>　　　<sub class="note-yuan">宣教郎</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">张康朝</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">重和元年<sub class="note-yuan">王昻榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">衞阗<sub class="note-yuan">字致虚仲达从祖终朝奉大夫绍兴倅赠太师魏国公</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">宣和元年<sub class="note-yuan">王俊又榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">衞肤敏<sub class="note-yuan">阗从子科第三人</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">宣和三年<sub class="note-yuan">何涣榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">富说<sub class="note-yuan">开弟</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">高宗建炎二年<sub class="note-yuan">李易榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">黄铨<sub class="note-yuan">甲科</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">高宗绍兴二年亲策进士张九成等读卷官欲以凌吴</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">夏孙彦朝参第一上曰士人初进便须别其忠佞九成</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">上自朕躬下至百执事言之无所畏避乃擢置首九成</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">自类试及亲策俱第一</span>

<span style="margin-left: 2em">进士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">洪武四年辛亥举会试壬子即罢科举谕中书省曰朕</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">设科举以求贤务得经明行修文质相称之士以资任</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">用今有司所取多后生少年观其文词亦若可用及试</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">用之不能措诸行事朕以实心求贤而天下以虚文应</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">朕非朕责实求贤之意今各处科举其暂停免令有司</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">察举贤才凡罢进士十有二年</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">十七年甲子颁行科举式凡三年一大比定于子午卯</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">酉年乡试辰戌丑未年会试吾郡登进士科自乙丑始</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">洪武十八年乙丑科<sub class="note-yuan">丁显榜</sub></span>

<span style="margin-left: 1em">月补之明年甲申会试礼部请取士之数　上问洪武</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">中所选几何尚书李至刚对曰各科不同多者四百七</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">十余人少者三十人　上曰朕即位姑率其多者不为</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">例至刚华亭人</span>

<span style="margin-left: 1em">高皇帝洪武三年下诏曰朕闻成周之制取才于贡士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">故贤者在职汉唐及宋科举取士各有定制然但贵词</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">章而不求德艺之实前元依古设科待士而权家势要</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">结纳奔竞辄窃仕禄贤者耻于并进甘隐山林风俗之</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">敝一至于此今朕统一华夷愿得贤人君子用之自今</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">八月为始特设科举以取怀才抱道之士务在经明行</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">修博通古今名实相称其中选者朕将亲策于廷第其</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">高下待以显擢</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">洪武三年庚戌科　是年以刘基秦裕伯为考官未及</span>
